# Multinationals with real R&D centers in Israel (not Israeli-founded, but
# real employers in the same job market, see
# https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_multinational_companies_with_research_and_development_centres_in_Israel).
# Expect mostly MISS: giants like these overwhelmingly run Workday/SAP
# SuccessFactors/custom systems, not the startup-oriented ATSes this
# project checks. Same gap as the Israeli banks/telecom/defense
# companies already in this file. A MISS here is real signal too.
# Real exceptions now: nvidia.com, paloaltonetworks.com, crowdstrike.com
# (added below), ebay.com, mastercard.com, appliedmaterials.com,
# broadcom.com, cisco.com, intel.com, visa.com, paypal.com, and
# salesforce.com all run
# Workday behind their own custom-branded careers page, which this
# project can't guess (no fixed slug the way Greenhouse/Lever have one)
# -- companies.yml's "workday" pins section hand-recovers each one, same
# idea as the existing Comeet pins just above it in that file. Checked
# and confirmed NOT reachable this way: google.com, apple.com,
# amazon.com, microsoft.com, meta.com, ibm.com, oracle.com, sap.com --
# all run fully custom/in-house systems with no Workday board at all.
# palantir.com needed no pin: it already resolves via the existing Lever
# guessing loop.

# Multinationals with an R&D centre in Israel, from Wikipedia's list of
# them (over 570 centres, ~130 named companies). 35 of the names were
# already here; these are the rest.
#
# Most of these will never resolve. A company this size runs its own
# careers stack, which is the whole finding from probing the tier-one
# giants: of five, only Amazon answered a plain request. They are listed
# anyway because a daily guess costs one budget each and the ones that
# DO sit on an ordinary ATS are exactly the ones we would otherwise
# never think to look for.
#
# Names, not guesses, is the point. This board's problem has never been
# the fetchers, it has been knowing which companies to ask about.

# Comeet-confirmed (pinned in companies.yml, found via search-indexed
# comeet.com/jobs URLs, not guessable from the domain alone)

# More from Built In's Israeli tech roundup, not already covered above

# From VC portfolio pages (Pitango, JVP, Viola) not already covered above

# Multinationals with real R&D centers in Israel (not Israeli-founded, but
# real employers in the same job market, see
# https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_multinational_companies_with_research_and_development_centres_in_Israel).
# Expect mostly MISS: giants like these overwhelmingly run Workday/SAP
# SuccessFactors/custom systems, not the startup-oriented ATSes this
# project checks. Same gap as the Israeli banks/telecom/defense
# companies already in this file. A MISS here is real signal too.
# Real exceptions now: nvidia.com, paloaltonetworks.com, crowdstrike.com
# (added below), ebay.com, mastercard.com, appliedmaterials.com,
# broadcom.com, cisco.com, intel.com, visa.com, paypal.com, and
# salesforce.com all run
# Workday behind their own custom-branded careers page, which this
# project can't guess (no fixed slug the way Greenhouse/Lever have one)
# -- companies.yml's "workday" pins section hand-recovers each one, same
# idea as the existing Comeet pins just above it in that file. Checked
# and confirmed NOT reachable this way: google.com, apple.com,
# amazon.com, microsoft.com, meta.com, ibm.com, oracle.com, sap.com --
# all run fully custom/in-house systems with no Workday board at all.
# palantir.com needed no pin: it already resolves via the existing Lever
# guessing loop.

# Gaming (missed category)

# Big corporates / defense / finance / retail / utilities / telecom:
# "Israeli lowtech": traditional large employers, not startups, but real
# hiring pipelines. Most of these will MISS on every fetcher here (large
# Israeli corporates mostly run SAP SuccessFactors, Oracle, iCIMS, or
# in-house ATSes, none of which are wired up). Kept in the batch anyway
# because a MISS is still signal (rules out the ATSes we do check) and
# because this is exactly the segment the original 35-domain seed list
# skipped entirely.

# Israeli-founded unicorns, TechAviv's curated list (Company/Website
# fields from TechAviv_Unicorn_List.2024-1-26.json, a snapshot of the
# same Airtable table techaviv.com/unicorns embeds, mirrored at
# github.com/TheBSD/scraping-israeli-data). Several of these are US/
# global-HQ'd with Israeli founders and may have zero current Israel-
# based listings. That's fine, same as any other company with no IL
# openings right now: they just contribute 0 rows to the default
# israel_only-scoped board until/unless one opens. 47 companies from
# this same list were already covered by earlier batches above; these
# 45 are the ones that weren't.

# US-Israel.co's Unicorn Tracker (usisrael.co/unicorn-tracker), companies
# not already covered by earlier batches. Checked live via HTTP before
# adding; excluded Vesttoo (vesttoo.com has no DNS records at all,
# dead domain, consistent with the company's 2023 fraud-scandal collapse).

# Vertex Ventures Israel's portfolio (vertexventures.co.il/portfolio) and
# poteto/hiring-without-whiteboards on GitHub (filtered to Israel-located
# entries only -- that list is global, not Israel-specific), companies not
# already covered by earlier batches. Every one below checked live via
# HTTP first; several plausible name-guesses were dropped after actually
# checking: Adaptive Shield, Identiq, and Kryon were each acquired and now
# just redirect to their acquirer's own site (CrowdStrike, Incode, and
# Nintex respectively) -- no longer independent hiring entities. Upswift
# was acquired by JFrog, already covered under its own domain above.
# Twistlock (acquired by Palo Alto Networks in 2019) looks defunct as a
# standalone site. Firedome's own domain has lapsed and is now squatted
# with gambling spam -- a live, current instance of the same "domain
# decayed" class of exclusion as Vesttoo above, not assumed from an old
# search snippet. EverAfter and Magical's first-guessed domains
# (everafter.com, magical.team) turned out to be unrelated sites (a
# Vegas wedding chapel, a dead parking page) -- everafter.ai and
# getmagical.com are their real ones.

# 2026 Israeli-funded-startup batch (BUGS OPENMARKET.pdf), domains
# verified live (DNS + a news/company-site hit) during triage, not just
# guessed from the company name. AITAN, Elio, Oak, and Skapion from the
# same list are left out -- no confidently-identified domain found for
# any of them (Elio and Oak in particular are too generic a name to
# isolate from same-named unrelated companies by search alone).

# Multinationals with an R&D centre in Israel, from Wikipedia's list of
# them (over 570 centres, ~130 named companies). 35 of the names were
# already here; these are the rest.

# giants: of five, only Amazon answered a plain request. They are listed
# anyway because a daily guess costs one budget each and the ones that
# DO sit on an ordinary ATS are exactly the ones we would otherwise
# never think to look for.
#
# Names, not guesses, is the point. This board's problem has never been
# the fetchers, it has been knowing which companies to ask about.

# Israeli company domains from Pvalyou's Israeli Startup Ecosystem
# snapshot (https://github.com/pvalyou/ise-data, snapshot/jobs.json,
# generatedAt 2026-09-22T12:08:31Z). A seed list of domains, nothing
# more. None of that file's 21,005 job rows is ingested, stored or
# republished here. Only the company domain crosses over, and every
# listing this project shows still comes from that company's own ATS
# through probe.py, the same as every other domain in this file.
# PRODUCT.md's whole claim is that a listing is ground-truthed against
# the employer's own API, and Pvalyou's rows are agent-collected from
# public records and news. That is the second-hand aggregation this
# project exists not to be, so the rows stay out and the domains come in.
#
# Licensed CC BY-NC 4.0. Quoting their LICENSE file directly, because
# the constraint should travel with the data rather than sit in a file
# nobody opens: "You may not use it commercially. Reselling the data, or
# building a paid product or service on it, is not permitted." Credit
# for the seed list belongs to Pvalyou (val@pvalyou.com handles
# commercial licensing). Anyone who later wants to put this project
# behind a paywall has to answer that sentence first.
#
# 3,987 of their 4,009 companies carried a parseable domain. 473 were
# already in this file or in the companies table, leaving 3,514 new.
# This first block is the 1,401 they tag "IT and Telecom", which between
# them account for 5,751 of the Israeli openings they counted. Tech
# first on purpose: if the nightly sweep only gets partway through the
# new arrivals, it has done the half worth doing.
#
# Expect a low hit rate, and do not read the 5,751 as a forecast. A
# random 100 of these domains resolved at 12%, so the honest projection
# is roughly 168 companies and somewhere between 760 and 1,100 Israeli
# jobs. Most large Israeli employers run SAP SuccessFactors, iCIMS or
# something in-house, none of which probe.py has a fetcher for, and a
# MISS here is the same real signal it is everywhere else in this file.

# found the board carrying Boeing, Leidos, Elbit and IAI and none of the

# nothing had tried them.

# workday-tenants.json holds RTX as tenant "globalhr", site
# REC_RTX_Ext_Gateway, 4,687 jobs seen, and Northrop as tenant "ngc",
# site Northrop_Grumman_External_Site, 3,784 seen. Both show zero on the
# board, which is the stranded-tenant shape fixed in e3bd1a7; they are
# listed here so the domain is right whichever route reaches them first.
#
# gd.com and generaldynamics.com answer 403 to us. Expect a MISS rather
# than a board: General Dynamics runs iCIMS, which has no fetcher here
# yet. They are listed so the miss is recorded against the real domain.
